data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { ClockSettings, TimeData } from './types';\nimport AnalogClock from './components/AnalogClock';\nimport ClockControls from './components/ClockControls';\n\nconst DEFAULT_SETTINGS: ClockSettings = {\n  theme: 'classic',\n  movement: 'smooth',\n  showDigital: true,\n  showDate: true,\n  showSeconds: true,\n  timeZoneOffset: 9, // 日本標準時 (JST, UTC+9)\n};\n\nexport default function App() {\n  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<ClockSettings>(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);\n\n  // 指定されたタイムゾーンオフセットから現在時刻情報を算出\n  const calculateTimeData = useCallback((offsetHours: number): TimeData => {\n    const now = new Date();\n    // UTCミリ秒を基準にオフセットを加算\n    const utcTime = now.getTime() + now.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000;\n    const targetDate = new Date(utcTime + offsetHours * 3600000);\n\n    const hours = targetDate.getHours();\n    const minutes = targetDate.getMinutes();\n    const seconds = targetDate.getSeconds();\n    const milliseconds = targetDate.getMilliseconds();\n\n    // 日付フォーマット (例: 10/24 (木))\n    const month = targetDate.getMonth() + 1;\n    const day = targetDate.getDate();\n    const days = ['日', '月', '火', '水', '木', '金', '土'];\n    const dayStr = days[targetDate.getDay()];\n    const dateString = `${month}/${day} (${dayStr})`;\n\n    // 時間フォーマット (HH:mm:ss)\n    const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');\n    const timeString = `${pad(hours)}:${pad(minutes)}:${pad(seconds)}`;\n\n    return {\n      hours,\n      minutes,\n      seconds,\n      milliseconds,\n      dateString,\n      timeString,\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  const [timeData, setTimeData] = useState<TimeData>(() =>\n    calculateTimeData(DEFAULT_SETTINGS.timeZoneOffset)\n  );\n\n  // requestAnimationFrame を用いたミリ秒単位のスムーズ更新ループ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let animId: number;\n\n    const tick = () => {\n      setTimeData(calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset));\n      animId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n    };\n\n    animId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(animId);\n  }, [settings.timeZoneOffset, calculateTimeData]);\n\n  const handleUpdateSettings = (partial: Partial<ClockSettings>) => {\n    setSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...partial }));\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"clock-app\">\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <h1 className=\"app-title\">Precision Analog Clock</h1>\n        <p className=\"app-subtitle\">高機能リアルタイム アナログ時計</p>\n      </header>\n\n      <main className=\"clock-display-wrapper\">\n        <AnalogClock settings={settings} timeData={timeData} />\n      </main>\n\n      <ClockControls\n        settings={settings}\n        onChangeSettings={handleUpdateSettings}\n      />\n\n      <footer className=\"clock-footer\">\n        <p>© 2025 Precision Clock App. Smooth [settings, setSettings] = useState<ClockSettings>(loadSavedSettings);\n\n  // Intl.DateTimeFormat を用いて夏時間を含む正確な地域日時を算出\n  const calculateTimeData = useCallback((offsetHours: number, ianaZone?: string): TimeData => {\n    const now = new Date();\n    const timeZone = ianaZone || OFFSET_TO_IANA[offsetHours] || 'UTC';\n\n    try {\n      const formatter = getFormatter(timeZone);\n      const parts = formatter.formatToParts(now);\n      const partMap: Record<string, string> = {};\n      for (const part of parts) {\n        partMap[part.type] = part.value;\n      }\n\n      const hours = parseInt(partMap.hour || '0', 10) % 24;\n      const minutes = parseInt(partMap.minute || '0', 10);\n      const seconds = parseInt(partMap.second || '0', 10);\n      const milliseconds = now.getMilliseconds();\n\n      const month = partMap.month || '1';\n      const day = partMap.day || '1';\n      const weekday = partMap.weekday || '';\n      const dateString = `${month}/${day} (${weekday})`;\n\n      const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');\n      const timeString = `${pad(hours)}:${pad(minutes)}:${pad(seconds)}`;\n\n      return {\n        hours,\n        minutes,\n        seconds,\n        milliseconds,\n        dateString,\n        timeString,\n      };\n    } catch {\n      // フォールバック処理\n      const hours = (now.getUTCHours() + offsetHours + 24) % 24;\n      const minutes = now.getUTCMinutes();\n      const seconds = now.getUTCSeconds();\n      const milliseconds = now.getMilliseconds();\n      const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');\n\n      return {\n        hours,\n        minutes,\n        seconds,\n        milliseconds,\n        dateString: `${now.getMonth() + 1}/${now.getDate()}`,\n        timeString: `${pad(hours)}:${pad(minutes)}:${pad(seconds)}`,\n      };\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const [timeData, setTimeData] = useState<TimeData>(() =>\n    calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana)\n  );\n\n  // 針の動作（smooth / step）に応じたタイマーループの最適化\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (settings.movement === 'smooth') {\n      let animId: number;\n      const tick = () => {\n        setTimeData(calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana));\n        animId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n      };\n\n      animId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n      return () => cancelAnimationFrame(animId);\n    } else {\n      // stepモード: 1秒ごとに更新し不要な描画負荷を防止\n      setTimeData(calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana));\n\n      let intervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval>;\n      const delayToNextSecond = 1000 - (Date.now() % 1000);\n\n      const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {\n        setTimeData(calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana));\n        intervalId = setInterval(() => {\n          setTimeData(calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana));\n        }, 1000);\n      }, delayToNextSecond);\n\n      return () => {\n        clearTimeout(timeoutId);\n        if (intervalId) clearInterval(intervalId);\n      };\n    }\n  }, [settings.movement, settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana, calculateTimeData]);\n\n  // 設定更新および localStorage への永続化\n  const handleUpdateSettings = (partial: Partial<ClockSettings>) => {\n    setSettings((prev) => {\n      const nextSettings = { ...prev, ...partial };\n      // タイムゾーンが変更された場合、対応するIANA識別子も同期\n      if (partial.timeZoneOffset !== undefined && !partial.iana) {\n        nextSettings.iana = OFFSET_TO_IANA[partial.timeZoneOffset] || prev.iana;\n      }\n      try {\n        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(nextSettings));\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('設定の保存に失敗しました:', err);\n      }\n      return nextSettings;\n    });\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"clock-app\">\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <h1 className=\"app-title\">Precision Analog Clock</h1>\n        <p className=\"app-subtitle\">高機能リアルタイム アナログ時計</p>\n      </header>\n\n      <main className=\"clock-display-wrapper\">\n        <AnalogClock settings={settings} timeData={timeData} />\n      </main>\n\n      <ClockControls\n        settings={settings}\n        onChangeSettings={handleUpdateSettings}\n      />\n\n      <footer className=\"clock-footer\">\n        <p>© 2025 Precision Clock App. Smooth Motion & Multi-Theme Engine.</p>\n      </footer>\n    </div>\n  );\n}","language":"tsx"}

event: file-complete

seconds * 6 + milliseconds * 0.006\n    : seconds * 6;\n  const minuteDeg = minutes * 6 + seconds * 0.1;\n  const hourDeg = (hours % 12) * 30 + minutes * 0.5;\n\n  // テーマクラス決定\n  const themeClass = `theme-${theme}`;\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={`analog-clock-container ${themeClass}`}\n      role=\"img\"\n      aria-label={`現在時刻: ${timeString}`}\n    >\n      <div className=\"clock-face\">\n        <div className=\"clock-dial\">\n          {/* 目盛り（親コンテナの中央50% 50%を回転中心として正確に円周配置） */}\n          {CLOCK_TICKS.map((tick) => (\n            <div\n              key={tick.id}\n              style={{\n                position: 'absolute',\n                top: 0,\n                left: 0,\n                width: '100%',\n                height: '100%',\n                transform: `rotate(${tick.angle}deg)`,\n                pointerEvents: 'none',\n              }}\n            >\n              <div className={tick.isHour ? data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/ClockControls.tsx","content":"import { ClockSettings, ClockTheme, HandMovement, TimeZoneOption } from '../types';\n\ninterface ClockControlsProps {\n  settings: ClockSettings;\n  onChangeSettings: (newSettings: Partial<ClockSettings>) => void;\n}\n\nconst TIMEZONES: TimeZoneOption[] = [\n  { label: '東京 (JST)', city: 'Asia/Tokyo', offset: 9 },\n  { label: 'シドニー (AEST)', city: 'Australia/Sydney', offset: 10 },\n  { label: 'ロンドン (GMT/UTC)', city: 'Europe/London', offset: 0 },\n  { label: 'パリ (CET)', city: 'Europe/Paris', offset: 1 },\n  { label: 'ニューヨーク (EST)', city: 'America/New_York', offset: -5 },\n  { label: 'ロサンゼルス (PST)', city: 'America/Los_Angeles', offset: -8 },\n];\n\nconst THEMES: { id: ClockTheme; label: string }[] = [\n  { id: 'classic', label: 'クラシック' },\n  { id: 'modern', label: 'モダン' },\n  { id: 'dark', label: 'ダーク' },\n  { id: 'gold', label: 'ゴールド' },\n];\n\nexport default function ClockControls({\n  settings,\n  onChangeSettings,\n}: ClockControlsProps) {\n  const { theme, movement, showDigital, showDate, showSeconds, timeZoneOffset, iana } = settings;\n\n  // IANA識別子優先、フォールバックで時差オフセットからタイムゾーンを特定\n  const selectedZone =\n    (iana && TIMEZONES.find((tz) => tz.city === iana)) ||\n    TIMEZONES.find((tz) => tz.offset === timeZoneOffset) ||\n    TIMEZONES[0];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"controls-panel\">\n      {/* 外観・地域設定セクション */}\n      <fieldset style={{ border: 'none', padding: 0, margin: 0, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>\n        <legend style={{ fontSize: '0.75rem', fontWeight: 600, color: '#94a3b8', letterSpacing: '0.05em', textTransform: 'uppercase', marginBottom: '2px' }}>\n          外観 & 地域\n        </legend>\n\n        {/* テーマ選択 */}\n        <div className=\"control-group\">\n          <label className=\"control-label\" id=\"theme-group-label\">テーマ</label>\n          <div className=\"theme-selector\" role=\"group\" aria-labelledby=\"theme-group-label\">\n            {THEMES.map((t) => (\n              <button\n                key={t.id}\n                type=\"button\"\n                className={`theme-btn ${theme === t.id ? タイムゾーンオフセットからIANAタイムゾーン文字列へのフォールバック辞書\nconst OFFSET_TO_IANA: Record<number, string> = {\n  9: 'Asia/Tokyo',\n  10: 'Australia/Sydney',\n  0: 'Europe/London',\n  1: 'Europe/Paris',\n  [-5]: 'America/New_York',\n  [-8]: 'America/Los_Angeles',\n};\n\n// Intl.DateTimeFormat のインスタンスキャッシュ\nconst formatterCache = new Map<string, Intl.DateTimeFormat>();\n\nfunction getFormatter(timeZone: string): Intl.DateTimeFormat {\n  let formatter = formatterCache.get(timeZone);\n  if (!formatter) {\n    formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ja-JP', {\n      timeZone,\n      month: 'numeric',\n      day: 'numeric',\n      weekday: 'short',\n      hour: 'numeric',\n      minute: 'numeric',\n      second: 'numeric',\n      hour12: false,\n    });\n    formatterCache.set(timeZone, formatter);\n  }\n  return formatter;\n}\n\nfunction loadSavedSettings(): ClockSettings {\n  try {\n    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n    if (saved) {\n      const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);\n now.getMilliseconds();\n\n      const month = partMap.month || '1';\n      const day = partMap.day || '1';\n      const weekday = partMap.weekday || '';\n      const dateString = `${month}/${day} minutes,\n        seconds,\n        milliseconds,\n        dateString,\n        timeString,\n      };\n    } catch {\n      // フォールバック処理\n      const hours = (now.getUTCHours() + offsetHours + 24) % 24;\n      const minutes = now.getUTCMinutes();\n      const seconds = now.getUTCSeconds();\n      const milliseconds = now.getMilliseconds();\n      const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');\n\n      return {\n        hours,\n        minutes,\n        seconds,\n        milliseconds,\n        dateString: `${now.getMonth() + 1}/${now.getDate()}`,\n        timeString: `${pad(hours)}:${pad(minutes)}:${pad(seconds)}`,\n      };\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const [timeData, setTimeData] = useState<TimeData>(() =>\n    calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana)\n  );\n\n  // 針の動作（smooth / step）に応じたタイマーループの最適化\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (settings.movement === 'smooth') {\n      let animId: number;\n      const tick = () => {\n        setTimeData(calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana));\n        animId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n      };\n\n      animId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);\n      return () => cancelAnimationFrame(animId);\n    } else {\n      // stepモード: 1秒ごとに更新し不要な描画負荷を防止\n      setTimeData(calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana));\n\n      let intervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval>;\n      const delayToNextSecond = 1000 - (Date.now() % 1000);\n\n      const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {\n        setTimeData(calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana));\n        intervalId = setInterval(() => {\n          setTimeData(calculateTimeData(settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana));\n        }, 1000);\n      }, delayToNextSecond);\n\n      return () => {\n        clearTimeout(timeoutId);\n        if (intervalId) clearInterval(intervalId);\n      };\n    }\n  }, [settings.movement, settings.timeZoneOffset, settings.iana, calculateTimeData]);\n\n  // 設定更新および localStorage への永続化\n  const handleUpdateSettings = (partial: Partial<ClockSettings>) => {\n    setSettings((prev) => {\n      const nextSettings = { ...prev, ...partial };\n      // タイムゾーンが変更された場合、対応するIANA識別子も同期\n      if (partial.timeZoneOffset !== undefined && !partial.iana) {\n        nextSettings.iana = OFFSET_TO_IANA[partial.timeZoneOffset] || prev.iana;\n      }\n      try {\n        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(nextSettings));\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('設定の保存に失敗しました:', err);\n      }\n      return nextSettings;\n    });\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"clock-app\">\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <h1 className=\"app-title\">Precision Analog Clock</h1>\n        <p className=\"app-subtitle\">高機能リアルタイム アナログ時計</p>\n      </header>\n\n      <main className=\"clock-display-wrapper\">\n        <AnalogClock settings={settings} timeData={timeData} />\n      </main>\n\n      <ClockControls\n        settings={settings}\n        onChangeSettings={handleUpdateSettings}\n      />\n\n      <footer className=\"clock-footer\">\n        <p>© 2025 Precision Clock App. Smooth Motion & Multi-Theme Engine.</p>\n      </footer>\n    </div>\n  );\n}","language":"tsx"}
